20 10:21:44      expertise.
21 10:21:44          Now, in terms of looking at programme updates and
22 10:21:47      the way that planners update programmes, it's quite easy
23 10:21:50      when you're updating programmes and you're going through
24 10:21:53      looking at steelwork or concrete works or ceiling works,
25 10:21:57      for example.  As a planner, you can go out on site and
                                      16
 1 10:22:03      you can look at things and physically go: yes, I can see

 3 10:22:10      complete or 20% complete.
 4 10:22:10          When it comes to MEP works, and especially testing
 5 10:22:12      and commissioning works, it's very difficult for
 6 10:22:14      planners to actually establish exact percentage
 7 10:22:17      completion in a programme, and therefore I would always
 8 10:22:20      err on the side of caution by looking at update
